2026-10-20
21 sources · 367 articles · 4,646,328 characters

In the Last 24 Hours

DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017 has surged to the top of the open-weight model rankings with a six-point jump on the Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index and a notable drop in hallucination rates, though cost-per-task comparisons still favour MiMo-V2.6-Pro and GPT-6.1 Sol. A 48-hour agent swarm attack on OpenCode dominated the security conversation, while Perplexity introduced a local-first mode that runs its Computer feature entirely on Apple silicon Macs with at least 36GB of unified memory, even offline. On the research front, NVIDIA unveiled Cosmos3-H-Dreams, a 16-billion-parameter model that generates real-time interactive simulations of clinical surgery from video and robot kinematics, and the Allen Institute for AI released FairStress, a benchmark testing whether LLMs apply demographic fairness consistently under persuasive pressure. IBM Research's ALTK-Evolve method pushed a basic GPT-4.1 ReAct agent to the top of the tau-squared-bench customer service leaderboard through learned memory alone, and Hugging Face launched TorchTPU, a native PyTorch backend that makes Google's TPUs a first-class device without JAX or torch_xla. Jeremy Scheff's widely shared essay "My obituary for programming" argued that AI coding agents are eroding the intellectual satisfaction that drew many people to software engineering in the first place.

Apple has set October 28 for a "Pro and tell" event with four new creative hardware products expected, while iPhone Duo orders are now capped at two per customer as scalpers resell units above five thousand dollars on eBay. The company announced that nearly 100 components across the iPhone 18 Pro and iPhone Duo are now made by American suppliers in more than a dozen states, including titanium from Pennsylvania and advanced films from Delaware. Samsung is reportedly planning six foldable phones next year to counter the iPhone Duo's momentum, and the HomeView smart home display finally launches November 13 after Apple confirmed that the new Siri AI's conversational abilities were the missing piece. Apple also expanded its Self Service Repair Store to cover the iPhone 18 Pro, M5 Max and Ultra Mac Studio, and M6 Mac mini, while Amazon discounts on AirPods Pro 3, the next-gen Apple TV 4K, and M5 MacBook Air models continue to draw attention.

On the software and legal side, iOS 27 introduces Impersonation Risk Detection to flag social engineering scams in real time, and iOS 27.2 adds Suggested Actions in Messages, FaceTime Dual Capture, Home app smart lock automations, and a split Up Next queue in Podcasts, while tvOS 27.2 brings sign language support and curated screen savers. Apple's acquisition spree this year, including MotionVFX, Color.io, and Samplab, is clearly aimed at expanding Apple Creator Studio, and new App Store chief Carson Oliver outlined a gaming strategy built on premium titles and Apple Arcade flexibility. The UK's Competition and Markets Authority has opened a consultation to force Apple to loosen its grip on the iPhone's NFC chip for third-party payment competitors, and Taction Technology is seeking ongoing royalties on iPhone and Apple Watch sales through 2035 following its 5.7 billion dollar Taptic Engine verdict. WhatsApp is developing an optional in-app browser for iPhone users, Apple's @ Work podcast highlighted the security risks of unpatched legacy devices, and Cloudflare and VoidZero announced tsrs, a Rust port of the TypeScript compiler that runs up to eight times faster with 70 percent less memory.

DeepSeek V4.1 Flash 1017 已經衝上開源模型榜首，喺 Artificial Analysis 智能指數跳升六分，幻覺率亦明顯下降，不過成本效益方面仲係輸畀 MiMo-V2.6-Pro 同 GPT-6.1 Sol。OpenCode 遭遇 48 小時 agent 群攻擊成為安全話題焦點，Perplexity 推出本地優先模式，令 Computer 功能可以完全喺 Apple Silicon Mac 上離線運行，最少要 36GB 統一記憶體。研究方面，NVIDIA 發表 Cosmos3-H-Dreams，一個 160 億參數模型可以從手術影片同機械人運動學即時生成互動式臨床模擬；Allen Institute 推出 FairStress 基準測試，檢驗大模型喺被說服壓力下會唔會一致咁應用人口統計公平性。IBM Research 嘅 ALTK-Evolve 方法靠學習記憶將一個普通 GPT-4.1 agent 推上 tau-squared-bench 客服排行榜頂峰，Hugging Face 推出 TorchTPU 原生 PyTorch 後端，使 Google TPU 唔使 JAX 都可以直接用。Jeremy Scheff 篇「我嘅程式設計訃告」引起廣泛討論，佢認為 AI 編碼代理正在侵蝕好多程式設計師最初入行嗰種智力挑戰嘅滿足感。

Apple 已經定咗 10 月 28 日開「Pro and tell」發布會，預計推出四款新創意硬件產品，而 iPhone Duo 已經限制每人最多買兩部，因為黃牛喺 eBay 炒到過五千美元。公司宣布 iPhone 18 Pro 同 iPhone Duo 接近 100 個零件已經由美國十幾個州嘅供應商生產，包括賓夕法尼亞嘅鈦合金同特拉華嘅先進薄膜。Samsung 據報明年要推出六款摺機嚟對抗 iPhone Duo 嘅勢頭，HomeView 智能家居顯示器終於 11 月 13 日上市，Apple 確認新 Siri AI 嘅對話能力先係令呢個產品可行嘅關鍵。Apple 亦將自助維修商店擴展到 iPhone 18 Pro、M5 Max 同 Ultra Mac Studio 同 M6 Mac mini，而 Amazon 上 AirPods Pro 3、新一代 Apple TV 4K 同 M5 MacBook Air 嘅折扣繼續吸引眼球。

軟件同法律方面，iOS 27 加入冒充風險偵測功能，可以即時標記社交工程詐騙；iOS 27.2 喺 Messages 加入建議操作、FaceTime 雙鏡頭拍攝、Home app 智能鎖自動化同 Podcasts 分割播放隊列，tvOS 27.2 則加入手語支援同自訂屏幕保護程序。Apple 今年一連串收購包括 MotionVFX、Color.io 同 Samplab，明顯係為咗擴展 Apple Creator Studio；新任 App Store 主管 Carson Oliver 講明遊戲策略係靠付費遊戲同 Apple Arcade 嘅靈活性。英國競爭同市場管理局已經展開諮詢，要逼 Apple 放開 iPhone NFC 晶片俾第三方支付競爭對手使用；Taction Technology 喺 57 億美元觸覺引擎專利勝訴之後，仲想追討 iPhone 同 Apple Watch 銷售分成直到 2035 年

iOS 27 adds scam-prevention feature to iPhone, here’s how to enable it

https://9to5mac.com/2026/10/20/ios-27-adds-scam-prevention-feature-to-iphone-heres-how-to-enable-it/📅 2026-10-21 02:56 UTC+8

iOS 27 introduces a new security feature called Impersonation Risk Detection that analyzes device and Apple Account data in real time to flag active social engineering scams, returning a risk level to apps so they can warn users or add extra verification steps. The feature is off by default and only works in supported apps, but it addresses a gap that traditional measures like two-factor authentication cannot cover, since the user is technically the one taking the action after being tricked. Users can enable it under Settings, Privacy & Security, by toggling on "Share with App Developers."

iOS 27 新出咗一個叫「冒充風險偵測」嘅保安功能，可以即時分析你部機同 Apple Account 嘅資料，判斷有冇人扮銀行或者政府機構嚟呃你轉賬或者改帳戶資料，然後畺個風險級別俾 app 決定要唔要彈警告或者加多一步驗證。呢個功能預設係關咗嘅，要自己去設定入面開，而且暫時淨係支援部分 app 先用得，不過佢解決咗一個好大嘅問題，就係就算你開咗兩步驗證，只要你自己俅人呃到親手做嘢，舊方法都擋唔住。

Google open-sources TorchTPU, a native PyTorch back end for TPUs

https://github.com/google-pytorch/torch_tpu📅 2026-10-21 00:40 UTC+8▲ 117 ptsHN discussion

Google has open-sourced TorchTPU, a native PyTorch backend that lets developers run PyTorch models directly on Google's Tensor Processing Units without relying on TensorFlow or the XLA compiler. The project ships custom ATen kernels, a compilation cache for optimized execution, and support for distributed training methods including data and tensor parallelism, with ready-made examples for models like Llama, Qwen, and ResNet. This is significant because it removes a major barrier for the PyTorch community wanting to leverage TPU hardware for large-scale training and inference.

Google 終於將 TorchTPU 開源咗，呢個係一個原生嘅 PyTorch 後端，可以直接喺 Google 嘅 TPU 上面跑 PyTorch 模型，唔使再繞路用 TensorFlow 或者 XLA。佢入面有自製嘅 ATen kernel、編譯快取、仲有支援數據並行同張量並行呢啲分佈式訓練方法，仲附帶咗 Llama、Qwen、ResNet 呢啲模型嘅範例。呢個對 PyTorch 社群嚟講好重要，因為以前想用 TPU 做訓練同推理真係好唔方便，而家終於可以直接上手。

EMudhra Intermediate CA Compromise

https://emudhra.we-have-a-problem.com/📅 2026-10-20 21:58 UTC+8▲ 342 ptsHN discussion

The private keys of eight eMudhra intermediate CA certificates have been compromised, giving an attacker the ability to impersonate any website or service on the internet. The compromised certificates chain to root stores trusted by Mozilla, Microsoft, Apple, and Chrome, meaning billions of users were potentially at risk before the issue was responsibly disclosed on 17 October 2026. Major browsers and operating system vendors have already blocked the affected certificates, but users must update their software immediately to ensure they are no longer trusting the compromised intermediates.

八張 eMudhra 中間 CA 證書嘅私鑰被人偷咗，即係話攻擊者可以冒充任何網站或者服務，影響範圍覆蓋 Mozilla、Microsoft、Apple 同 Chrome 所有主要平台，涉及幾十億用戶。事件喺 2026 年 10 月 17 號被負責任地披露，各大瀏覽器同作業系統已經即時封鎖咗有問題嘅證書，但係用家一定要即刻更新自己部機同軟件先至安全。

How I got over 300 Tor exit nodes removed

https://kaba.cx/posts/20261020_Tor/📅 2026-10-20 21:39 UTC+8▲ 214 ptsHN discussion

On October 2, 2026, 333 Tor exit nodes — roughly 10% of all exit relays — were found to be compromised with the Ebury 1.8.3 OpenSSH backdoor, which redirected SSH connections to attacker-controlled servers to steal passwords and inject malware. The author identified the infected nodes by systematically testing every exit relay with a custom script and reported the findings to the Tor Project's network health team, who flagged and shut down all compromised servers within three days. The incident underscores that Tor relays are increasingly targeted as attack infrastructure and that users should disable password-based SSH authentication in favour of hardware keys.

2026年10月2號，有333個Tor出口節點被發現感染咗Ebury 1.8.3後門，即係成個網絡大約一成嘅出口中咗招，攻擊者會將SSH連線偷偷轉去自己嘅伺服器，偷你嘅密碼再植入惡意軟件。作者用自製腳本逐個出口節點測試，搵出所有被感染嘅節點之後通知Tor網絡健康團隊，三日之內全部有問題嘅伺服器都被關咗。呢單嘢話畀我哋知，Tor節點已經成為攻擊者嘅目標，用SSH金鑰而唔係密碼先至係正路。

https://www.quantamagazine.org/triplet-of-ghostly-galaxies-hints-at-what-dark-matter-is-and-isnt-20261014/📅 2026-10-17 23:33 UTC+8▲ 71 ptsHN discussion

Three unusually dim galaxies located 65 million light-years away — DF2, DF4, and DF9 — appear to contain almost no dark matter, a finding that has puzzled astronomers since the first was discovered in 2018. A new study suggests they were born from a collision between two ordinary galaxies in which dark matter passed straight through while gas and dust clumped together to form new stars. This separation of dark matter from ordinary matter provides strong evidence that dark matter is a real physical substance rather than merely a modification of gravity, dealing a significant blow to alternative gravity theories.

喺6500萬光年外有三個好暗淡嘅星系，叫做DF2、DF4同DF9，佢哋幾乎冇暗物質，由2018年發現第一個開始就搞到天文學家好困惑。最新研究話呢三個星系可能係兩個普通星系撞埋一齊之後形成嘅，撞嗰陣暗物質直接穿過去，但普通物質就聚埋一齊形成新嘅星。呢個發現好有力咁證明暗物質係真實存在嘅物質，唔係淨係改一改萬有引力定律就可以解釋到，對嗰啲修正引力理論係好大嘅打擊。

NVIDIA Cosmos3-H-Dreams: Bringing Clinical Surgery to Life Through Generative Simulation

https://huggingface.co/blog/nvidia/cosmos3-h-dreams📅 2026-10-21 02:17 UTC+8

NVIDIA has introduced Cosmos3-H-Dreams, a 16-billion-parameter generative AI model that produces real-time, interactive simulations of clinical cholecystectomy by learning visual dynamics directly from surgical video and robot kinematics rather than relying on traditional numerical physics models. The system uses a teacher-to-student distillation pipeline with FlashDreams streaming to achieve low-latency generation that responds coherently to instrument commands, handling complex challenges like tissue deformation, occlusion, and temporal consistency within the human body. NVIDIA emphasizes the model remains a research simulator and not a validated clinical tool, though it opens paths toward full-procedure coverage, hybrid numerical-generative simulation, and autonomous surgical policy training.

NVIDIA 推出咗 Cosmos3-H-Dreams，呢個係一個 160 億參數嘅生成式 AI 模型，可以即時模擬臨床膽囊切除手術嘅畫面，唔使靠傳統物理模擬，直接由手術影片同機械人動作數據學出嚟。佢用咗 teacher-to-student 蒸餾技術配合 FlashDreams 串流推理，令模型可以即時回應手術器械指令，同時處理到體內組織變形、遮擋同時間一致性呢啲複雜問題。不過 NVIDIA 強調呢個只係研究用嘅模擬器，仲未係可以用喺病人身上嘅臨床工具，但已經為日後做全手術覆蓋、混合模擬同自主手術策略訓練打好咗基礎。

FairStress: A deeper evaluation of fairness in LLM decision-making

https://huggingface.co/blog/allenai/fairstress📅 2026-10-21 00:09 UTC+8

Researchers at the Allen Institute for AI have released FairStress, a benchmark that tests whether large language models apply demographic fairness consistently when their decisions are challenged by persuasive arguments, not just whether they produce the same final answer across identity-swapped scenarios. Across 15 models and over 13 million test cases spanning hiring, lending, healthcare, and other domains, the team found a pattern they call anti-stereotypical over-correction, where models were more willing to abandon a clearly correct decision when doing so favored a minority-coded candidate. The findings matter because LLMs are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings where inconsistent application of evidence based on demographic identity can directly affect people's access to jobs, loans, and medical care.

艾倫人工智能研究所推出咗 FairStress，呢個基準唔係淨係睇模型最終揀邊個，而係測試當有人用說服性論據挑戰模型嘅決定時，佢哋會唔會因為候選人嘅種族、性別等身份而唔同程度地放棄原本正確嘅判斷。研究發現一種叫「反刻板印象過度修正」嘅現象，就係模型喺放棄正確答案會令少數群體受益嘅時候，明顯更容易改主意。呢個發現好重要，因為而家好多招聘、貸款、醫療等關鍵決策都開始用大語言模型，如果佢哋對唔同背景嘅人用唔同標準，就會直接影響到人哋嘅機會同資源。

ALTK-Evolve: How learned memory tops the τ²-bench leaderboard on customer service tasks without a frontier model

https://huggingface.co/blog/ibm-research/altk-evolve-bc📅 2026-10-20 23:19 UTC+8

IBM Research's ALTK-Evolve method takes a basic ReAct agent running on GPT-4.1 and pushes it to the top of the τ²-bench customer service leaderboard by distilling past task attempts into short behavioral guidelines appended to the system prompt, achieving 67.5% Pass^4 on retail tasks without any weight updates or human annotation. The approach shows that learned memory can outperform frontier models on policy-bound agent tasks, and that the optimal amount of guidance varies by domain — retail benefits from more rules while airline reliability peaks with a compact high-confidence core. This matters for enterprises seeking cost-effective, self-improving agents that do not depend on the most expensive models.

IBM Research 嘅 ALTK-Evolve 方法將 GPT-4.1 驅動嘅基本 ReAct agent 推上 τ²-bench 客服排行榜榜首，做法係將過去任務嘅成功同失敗提煉成短小嘅行為指引，附加喺 system prompt 入面，零售任務達到 67.5% Pass^4，完全唔使更新模型權重或者人工標註。呢個方法證明咗學習記憶可以喺政策約束嘅 agent 任務上打贏 frontier model，而且唔同領域需要唔同份量嘅指引——零售越多越好，但航空嘅可靠性反而用少量高信心規則先至最好。對企業嚟講，呢代表可以用更平、可自我改進嘅 agent 取代最貴嘅模型。

MPS in PyTorch 2.15

https://huggingface.co/blog/mps-pytorch-2-15📅 2026-10-20 08:00 UTC+8

PyTorch 2.15 brings major improvements to the MPS backend on Apple silicon Macs, including FP8 tensor support, 5 to 6.5 times faster decoding for mixture-of-experts language models, and a dramatic reduction in video generation memory from 270 GiB down to 8 GiB. The release also adds native grouped matrix multiplication, faster linear algebra operations, and specialized kernels for audio and training workloads. These changes collectively make the Mac a far more capable platform for running and training modern AI models locally.

This shift brings eager execution, torch.compile, and native distributed APIs like FSDP and tensor parallelism to Trainium, with 89% of the top 100 text-generation architectures now supported out of the box compared to 46% previously. The change means developers can use the same code and scripts they already know for GPUs, dramatically lowering the barrier to training and inference on AWS's cost-effective AI accelerators.

The Agent Said It Was Done. The Database Disagreed.

https://huggingface.co/blog/microsoft/thinkingbox📅 2026-10-04 06:56 UTC+8

Microsoft's ThinkingBox benchmark grades AI agents on the actual database state they leave behind rather than their generated text or tool calls, revealing that 67% of failed attempts still appeared to terminate cleanly. Across 507 business workflows run 20 times each, the benchmark exposes a critical gap between what a model can do once and what it does reliably every time, with models like Kimi-K3 solving 94% of tasks at least once but only 13% consistently. For anyone deploying agents against real records, this means headline accuracy scores are misleading and consistency is the metric that actually matters.

微軟出咗個叫 ThinkingBox 嘅 benchmark，唔係睇 AI agent 講咩或者做咗幾多次 tool call，而係直接查資料庫入面嘅最終狀態，結果發現超過六成嘅失敗個案表面上睇落好似成功咁。佢哋將 507 個商業工作流程重複跑 20 次，發現好多模型「做得到一次」同「每次都做得到」之間差距好大，例如 Kimi-K3 有 94% 嘅任務至少成功過一次，但得 13% 可以 20 次全部做啱。如果你真係要將 AI agent 接駁去真實嘅資料庫做嘢，單睇 pass@1 嗰啲分數係會誤導你嘅，穩定性先至係最關鍵嘅指標。

AutoSynthData: Generating Training Data for Enterprise Agents

https://huggingface.co/blog/ServiceNow-AI/autosynthdata📅 2026-10-02 12:01 UTC+8

ServiceNow CoreAI has introduced AutoSynthData, a pipeline that automatically generates training data for enterprise AI agents by analyzing a target model's failures in a given environment and using a stronger teacher model to create new tasks that specifically exercise the capabilities the agent lacks. The system works in two phases—target generation and multiplication—producing validated, diverse tasks that are feasible, realistic, and calibrated to the model's current weaknesses. This approach addresses a core challenge in enterprise AI: turning scattered agent failures into large-scale, high-quality training datasets that close the gap between general model capability and the specific demands of a company's systems and workflows.

ServiceNow CoreAI 推出咗 AutoSynthData，呢套系統可以自動為企業 AI agent 生成訓練數據，方法係分析目標模型喺特定環境入面嘅失敗案例，再用一個更強嘅 teacher model 去創造新任務，專攻模型未掌握嘅能力。成個流程分兩個階段，先係 target generation 生成核心樣本，再透過 multiplication 擴展成大量變體，每個任務都要通過可行性、真實性同驗證器嘅檢查。呢個方法解決咗企業 AI 一個核心難題：點樣將零散嘅 agent 失敗變成大規模、高質素嘅訓練數據，令模型真正適應公司嘅系統同工作流程。

https://huggingface.co/blog/open-tts-leaderboard📅 2026-09-30 08:00 UTC+8

Researchers have launched the Open TTS Leaderboard, a new evaluation platform that uses objective metrics like word error rate, inference speed, and speaker similarity to rank open-source multilingual text-to-speech models. Getting the Source Right, Not Just the Fact: Source-Aware Verification for MCP Agents

https://huggingface.co/blog/MultiverseComputingCAI/getting-the-source-right-not-just-the-fact-source📅 2026-09-29 21:07 UTC+8

ProvenanceGuard is a new verification layer for LLM agents that use the Model Context Protocol to gather information from multiple tools, designed to catch a subtle failure mode called cross-source conflation, where a fact is true but wrongly attributed to the wrong source. Unlike existing checkers that pool all evidence together, it tracks source identity through every step of verification and can block or repair answers that misattribute claims. In tests on medical agent traces, it caught 138 of 139 claims that human experts said should be blocked, outperforming four source-blind baselines.

ProvenanceGuard 係一個用嚟驗證 MCP agent 答案嘅新系統，佢唔係淨係睇事實啱唔啱，仲要查清楚每個論點係咪真係嚟自佢講嗰個來源，解決咗「事實冇錯但歸錯來源」呢個好危險嘅問題。佢將來源身份一路帶住做驗證，唔會好似其他工具咁將所有證據混埋一齊算。喺醫療 agent 嘅測試入面，139 個專家認為應該擋住嘅論點佢成功擋咗 138 個，表現好過現有嘅驗證方法。

Meta pauses Muse sharing after researchers find AI chats on the web

https://www.theverge.com/tech/1014627/meta-muse-ai-sharing-ai-chats📅 2026-10-21 02:07 UTC+8

Meta has paused the sharing feature on its Muse AI assistant after security firm Claroc discovered over 6,800 Muse conversation links exposed through public DNS records, revealing sensitive information including a marketing company's revenue review, a loan application, and a workplace's full-year staff calendar. The links were easily discoverable because Meta built each URL from the conversation's title and a random ID, allowing researchers to reconstruct them from DNS data alone. The incident adds to a growing pattern of AI chatbots like ChatGPT, Claude, and Grok inadvertently making private conversations searchable on the web.

Meta 暫停咗 Muse AI 助手嘅分享功能，因為保安公司 Claroc 發現超過六千八百條 Muse 對話連結可以透過公開 DNS 記錄搵到，入面有公司收入報告、貸款申請同埋公司全年員工日曆等敏感資料。問題出喺 Meta 設計連結嘅方式太容易被人逆向工程，用對話標題同隨機 ID 就組合成網址，等於將私隱完全暴露咗。呢單嘢再次反映 AI 聊天工具嘅分享功能好容易令私人對話變成人人可睇嘅公開內容。

The fossil fuel comeback is here

https://www.theverge.com/science/1013954/fossil-fuel-power-infrastructure-data-centers-report📅 2026-10-20 05:48 UTC+8

The US is building more fossil fuel infrastructure than it has in a decade, driven by surging electricity demand from AI data centers and a Trump administration actively undermining wind and solar through stop work orders, blocked permits, and removed tax incentives. Plans for new fossil fuel capacity jumped from 5 percent of all new capacity in 2024 to 30 percent by mid-2026, and if the pipeline is completed, it could lock in higher pollution and electricity prices for decades. The shift is notable because renewables remain cheaper than new gas plants, yet renewable projects are being canceled at nearly twice the rate of fossil fuel projects.

美國而家正起緊十年嚟最多嘅化石燃料基建，主要係因為AI數據中心帶嚟嘅用電量飆升，加上特朗普政府用各種手段打壓風電同太陽能項目。新化石燃料發電嘅計劃由2024年只佔新增容量嘅5%飆升到2026年中期嘅30%，雖然可再生能源其實仲平過天然氣，但可再生能源項目被取消嘅速度反而係化石燃料項目嘅接近兩倍。呢個趨勢如果繼續落去，美國未來幾十年可能要承受更高嘅污染同電費。

Expanding our defenses against financial scam ads

https://blog.google/products/ads-commerce/financial-services-verification-united-states-canada/📅 2026-10-20 23:00 UTC+8

Google is expanding its Financial Services Verification program to the United States and Canada in early 2027, requiring financial advertisers to prove they are authorized by regulators before running ads. Benchmarking 12 HTML-to-PDF APIs Across 14 Rendering Checks

https://hackernoon.com/benchmarking-12-html-to-pdf-apis-across-14-rendering-checks📅 2026-10-20 22:58 UTC+8

A team benchmarked 12 HTML-to-PDF generation APIs by sending the same 4-page invoice through each and checking the output against 14 rendering criteria covering layout, JavaScript, international text, page breaks, and fillable forms. PDFGate and DocRaptor were the only two to pass all 14 checks, while others failed on issues ranging from missing page numbers to broken emoji rendering and empty text layers. The full test data, HTML, and output PDFs are publicly available for anyone to reproduce.

有人用同一份四頁 invoice 去測十二個 HTML 轉 PDF 嘅 API，逐頁核對十四項渲染檢查，包括版面、JavaScript、多國語言、分頁同可填寫嘅表單欄位。結果得 PDFGate 同 DocRaptor 全部十四項都過，其他 API 分別喺頁碼、emoji、文字層等地方出問題。所有測試數據同輸出 PDF 都公開喺 GitHub，Anyone 可以自己重跑一次。

Your AI Agent Is Only as Smart as Your Data Pipeline

https://hackernoon.com/your-ai-agent-is-only-as-smart-as-your-data-pipeline📅 2026-10-20 21:30 UTC+8

The article argues that an AI agent's performance ceiling is determined by its data pipeline rather than the model itself, since agents confidently act on stale, inconsistent, or poorly structured data without the human instinct to question it. The author identifies four common pipeline failures — stale data, conflicting definitions, unstructured content, and missing lineage — and recommends practical fixes like data contracts, freshness thresholds, permission filtering before retrieval, and full trace logging. The core takeaway is that teams should look one layer below the model before blaming hallucinations, because in most cases the agent did exactly what it was asked with exactly what it was given.

篇文章嘅核心論點係，AI agent 嘅表現上限其實係由數據管道決定，唔係模型本身，因為 agent 會好自信噉用過期、定義衝突或者結構混亂嘅數據嚟回答問題，唔會好似人類咁覺得有啲唔對路。作者指出四種常見嘅管道問題——數據過期、定義不一致、非結構化內容同缺乏溯源——並建議用數據契約、時效閾值、檢索前權限過濾同完整日誌嚟解決。一句講晒，下次 agent 講錯嘢之前，不如先睇下佢畀到嘅數據有冇問題，好大機會模型本身冇錯，錯喺畀佢嘅輸入。

The key takeaway is that most developers lose time on infrastructure rather than the model itself, and that hosted APIs remain the cheaper option below roughly ten million requests a month. For teams wanting data inside their own network, one Docker command on one GPU is the practical sweet spot before any cluster work is needed.

呢篇文講點樣最快將開源AI模型推上production，由改一行base URL接OpenAI兼容endpoint，到用Docker喺一張GPU上self-host，再到需要autoscaling先上Kubernetes，層層遞進講得好清楚。最重點係好多developer其實唔係卡喺模型本身，而係卡喺揀硬件、download weights、cold start呢啲嘢上面，而且流量未去到每月一千萬request嘅話，用hosted API其實仲平過自己養GPU。如果數據一定要留喺自己network入面，一張GPU加一個Docker container就係最實際嘅起步點，唔使一上嚟就搞cluster。

https://hackernoon.com/revisiting-ml-model-selection-with-an-external-realmlp-challenger📅 2026-10-20 17:13 UTC+8

A financial machine learning team decided to test RealMLP, a model from a NeurIPS 2024 paper, as an external challenger alongside their internally selected models for trading signal evaluation. Although RealMLP showed only moderate standalone performance, it ultimately joined the final ensembles in both their Intra and Swing tracks because it contributed information that the existing models did not. The article proposes a "Claim-to-Send Gate" workflow to validate factual claims in AI-generated customer emails before they reach a recipient. The gate requires five checks — identifying material claims, matching each to current evidence, verifying audience appropriateness, assigning approval ownership, and confirming the final send state — because AI tools can produce fluent but unsupported commitments that are easy to overlook. 呢篇文章提出咗一個叫「Claim-to-Send Gate」嘅流程，用嚟喺AI生成嘅客戶郵件發出之前，逐句核實入面嘅事實聲明同承諾。個流程有五步檢查：搵出所有實質聲明、配對可靠證據、確認收件人係咪啱、指定邊個有權批准、同埋確認最終發出嘅版本。重點係AI寫嘅嘢睇落好順好專業，但可能完全冇證據支持，一旦客戶信咗就出問題。

https://hackernoon.com/ai-alone-wont-fix-your-legacy-system-migration-strategy📅 2026-10-20 15:30 UTC+8

Legacy system migration remains fundamentally an architecture problem that requires human judgment on business criticality, risk, and strategy, even as AI accelerates code analysis, documentation, and dependency mapping. While AI can process vast codebases far faster than any human team, it cannot decide whether a component should be rebuilt, retired, or retained, because those calls depend on compliance, cost, and organisational context that no model can determine from source code alone. Organisations that hand migration strategy to AI risk carrying technical debt into new environments while losing accountability over the decisions that actually matter.

遷移舊系統從來都唔係單純寫代码嘅問題，而係架構決策問題，AI 可以幫你分析代码、生成文檔、搵出依賴關係，但最終邊個組件要重建、邊個要淘汰、邊個要保留，呢啲決定始終要由人做。AI 處理海量代码嘅速度遠超人類團隊，但佢唔識判斷業務重要性、合規要求同可接受風險，呢啲嘢唔係睇源碼就得出嚟。如果將遷移策略交俾 AI，好大機會將技術債帶入新環境，同時失去對關鍵決策嘅把關能力。

Stop Writing Camera Moves Into Your AI Video Prompts. Film Them Instead.

https://hackernoon.com/stop-writing-camera-moves-into-your-ai-video-prompts-film-them-instead📅 2026-10-20 11:50 UTC+8

AI video tools like Kling now let filmmakers feed a short reference clip filmed on a phone as the motion source instead of describing camera moves in text prompts, which solves the problem of compound or complex camera movements that models consistently fail to generate from words alone. The key insight is that a prompt describes an outcome while a reference clip provides the actual physics of the move, and creators report going from dozens of failed generations to a usable take on the first try once they switch to filming. This shift matters because on a shot list with many moving shots, it turns a lost week into a lost afternoon.

而家 Kling 呢啲 AI 影片工具已經可以直接用手機拍一段短片做動作參考，唔使再靠文字描述鏡頭運動，尤其係弧線、手持、複合運鏡呢啲 prompt 點寫都唔掂嘅嘢，拍一段入去一次就搞掂。重點係 prompt 只係講個結果，但參考片直接畀模型知道鏡頭行邊條路、幾快、幾時停，所以複雜嘅鏡頭用拍嘅比用寫嘅快好多，一單鏡頭由幾十次失敗變做一次就成功。

Cloudflare Blog 20 articles

Last 24 Hours

Cloudflare and VoidZero have announced tsrs, a Rust port of the TypeScript compiler developed in collaboration with the TypeScript team and incubated under the Oxc toolchain. Early benchmarks show the new compiler is up to 8x faster and uses roughly 70% less memory than TypeScript 7.0, with the TypeScript team retaining full control over the language's direction.

https://blog.cloudflare.com/typescript-rust-port/📅 2026-10-20 21:00 UTC+8

The biggest wins came from fixing a prompt cache bug that prevented shared prefixes from being reused across repositories and from reducing the number of LLM calls per session, with the remaining savings coming from model and effort-level tuning. The work shows that even with high cache hit rates, uncontrolled token growth can silently double your inference bill, and that systematic measurement of where tokens actually go is the first step to fixing it.

The fix highlights the subtle complexities of building a connection pooler that must translate between different client and server protocol expectations while caching responses globally.

The keys to the Internet change on October 11. Are you ready?

https://blog.cloudflare.com/root-ksk-2024-rollover/📅 2026-10-07 01:50 UTC+8

On October 11, 2026, the DNS root will perform its second-ever key-signing key rollover, replacing KSK-2017 with KSK-2024, a change that anchors the entire DNSSEC chain of trust. If a validating resolver fails to trust the new key before the switch, users of that resolver could be unable to reach any website under any top-level domain. Most website operators need take no action, and Cloudflare's 1.1.1.1 and Gateway DNS already trust the new key, but anyone running a DNSSEC-validating resolver should verify readiness using the published sentinel test.

2026年10月11號，DNS root 會進行第二次 key-signing key 轉換，用 KSK-2024 取代 KSK-2017，呢個 key 係成個 DNSSEC 信任鏈嘅起點。如果 resolver 喺轉換之前冇信任新 key，用嗰個 resolver 嘅用戶就會連唔到任何網站。大部分網站營運者唔使做嘢，用 Cloudflare 1.1.1.1 或者 Gateway DNS 嘅人亦都唔使理，但係自己跑 DNSSEC-validating resolver 嘅人就要即刻去測試睇下 ready 未。

Read full article →

Why AlphaFold Didn't Solve Protein Folding — Pushmeet Kohli, Google DeepMind & Sal Candido, Biohub

https://www.latent.space/p/biohub-deepmind📅 2026-10-10 08:31 UTC+8

Google DeepMind's Pushmeet Kohli and Biohub's Sal Candido argue that AlphaFold's breakthrough was only the beginning of AI-driven biology, not the end of the protein folding problem. They emphasize that scaling compute and data alone won't solve biology — finding the right scaling laws, prioritizing good data over more data, and moving from individual proteins to whole biological systems are the real challenges ahead. The panel suggests that curing all disease requires 10x breakthroughs rather than incremental improvements, and that protein language models may already contain scientific knowledge we haven't yet unlocked.

Google DeepMind嘅Pushmeet Kohli同Biohub嘅Sal Candido喺呢個panel入面講到，AlphaFold雖然係一個重大突破，但蛋白質摺疊問題根本仲未解決，AI做生物學仲有好多嘢要搞。佢哋認為淨係靠堆算力同堆數據係唔夠㗎，最緊要係搵到啱嘅scaling law、用啱嘅數據，仲要由研究單一蛋白質進展到理解成個生物系統。佢哋話要根治所有疾病，唔係靠10%嘅漸進改善，而係要10倍甚至100倍嘅突破先至得。

Read full article →

Simon Willison 出咗 llm-gemini 0.35，加入咗 Google 新出嘅 Gemini 4 Argon 模型支援。Argon 定價係每百萬輸入 token 兩蚊、輸出十蚊，同 GPT-6 Sol 一樣平，仲平過 Claude Opus 5.5 一半，不過 12 月 1 號就會加價一倍。根據 Artificial Analysis 嘅 benchmark 數據，Argon 喺 agentic 工作負載方面性價比好高，Gemini 喺 SVG 繪圖方面繼續係最強。

This matters for anyone maintaining Python projects that target multiple versions, as it shifts the minimum baseline and unlocks modern language capabilities.

Profiling web-edit with Python 3.15: flame graphs and source heatmaps

https://simonwillison.net/2026/Oct/12/python-3-15-web-edit-profiling/📅 2026-10-13 00:58 UTC+8

The profiling also revealed that 95.1% of CPU stacks originated from AnyIO's worker thread, meaning a main-thread-only profile would have missed the vast majority of performance data. This demonstrates the practical value of Python 3.15's new profiling tools for identifying real-world bottlenecks that older approaches would overlook.

How Stripe uses graph search and state machines to auto-remediate a global database fleet

https://stripe.com/blog/how-stripe-uses-graph-search-and-state-machines-to-auto-remediate-a-global-database-fleet📅 2026-07-16 08:00 UTC+8

Stripe modeled its MongoDB infrastructure as a traversable graph and applied pathfinding algorithms to automatically compute and execute recovery plans for unhealthy database shards. This system cut pager volume by 30 percent, roughly 200 pages per year, and eliminated 12 days of unhealthy shard states annually. The approach also allows Stripe to roll out new shard layouts without any manual intervention.

Ontology-Driven Observability: Building an End-to-End Knowledge Graph at Netflix Scale

https://netflixtechblog.com/ontology-driven-observability-building-an-end-to-end-knowledge-graph-at-netflix-scale-421392cf5adf📅 2026-10-17 00:01 UTC+8

Netflix engineers have built an ontology-driven knowledge graph to solve the problem of cross-system observability at their massive scale, where a single regression investigation once took four hours because different tools disagreed on what entities were and how they related. The solution, called the Observability Ontology, uses W3C standards like RDF and SPARQL to create a machine-readable agreement across their telemetry, alerting, incident, and deployment systems, turning unstructured operational data into deterministic triples that can be queried in seconds. The approach is notable because it uses stochastic tools like LLMs and fuzzy matching to populate the graph, but the ontology layer guarantees consistent, reproducible answers.

Netflix 的工程團隊為咗解決佢哋超大规模系統入面跨工具嘅可觀測性問題，起咗一個基於本體論嘅知識圖譜，因為以前一次簡單嘅效能倒退都要花四粒鐘先搵到根因，原因就是唔同系統對同一個嘢用唔同嘅名同定義。佢哋用 W3C 標準（RDF、OWL、SPARQL）建立咗一個機器可讀嘅統一協議，將遙測數據、告警、事故同部署資訊變成確定性嘅三元組，一次查詢就搞掂以前要跨幾個工具先搵到嘅答案。呢個方法嘅亮點係用咗 LLM 同模糊匹配呢啲非確定性工具去填充圖譜，但本體論層確保每次查詢結果都係一致同可重現嘅。

Integrating Netflix’s Foundation Model into Personalization applications

https://netflixtechblog.com/integrating-netflixs-foundation-model-into-personalization-applications-cf176b5860eb📅 2026-10-15 04:02 UTC+8

Netflix engineers have detailed three distinct approaches for integrating their Foundation Model into production personalization systems: embeddings via a dedicated Embedding Store, subgraph integration where the model becomes part of a downstream model's graph, and direct fine-tuning similar to how LLMs are adapted for domain-specific tasks. Each method offers different trade-offs between ease of integration, freshness of recommendations, and computational cost, with embeddings serving as the low-barrier starting point and subgraph or fine-tuning reserved for high-impact use cases that justify the added complexity. This work addresses a notable gap in the literature, which has focused heavily on training large models but offered little practical guidance on how to deploy them efficiently within existing production pipelines.

Netflix 嘅工程團隊分享咗點樣將佢哋嘅 Foundation Model 接入到實際嘅個人化推薦系統入面，總共有三種做法：用 embeddings 做特徵、將模型做做下游模型嘅 subgraph、同埋直接 fine-tune 成自己用。每種方法都有唔同嘅取捨，embeddings 最簡單但可能會有延遲問題，subgraph 同 fine-tuning 效果最好但成本同複雜度都高好多。呢篇文嘅重點係佢哋填咗一個好大嘅空白，因為之前好多研究都集中喺點樣訓練大模型，但係好少人講點樣將呢啲模型實際落地到生產環境入面。

The new architecture matches client events against ad-serving responses within a 60-minute window, removes the registry from the serving path entirely, and scales elastically without downtime.

Staying Evergreen: How we automated secure access and repo hygiene in a shared org

https://github.blog/engineering/platform-security/staying-evergreen-how-we-automated-secure-access-and-repo-hygiene-in-a-shared-org/📅 2026-10-16 02:00 UTC+8

GitHub built Evergreen, a GitHub App that automates quarterly access reviews for outside collaborators and detects stale repositories for archival in a shared organization with over 1,400 employees. The tool follows a "fail secure" principle where unresponded reviews result in automatic revocation while never deleting anything, and tracks all state as JSON files in git so the commit history doubles as an audit trail. A careful rollout using dry-run mode and heavy overcommunication made the first automated run a complete non-event across the organization.

GitHub 開發咗一個叫 Evergreen 嘅 GitHub App，用嚟自動做每季外部協作者嘅權限審查同埋偵測長期冇活動嘅 repo 再歸檔。佢嘅核心原則是 fail secure，冇人回應就自動撤權，但永遠唔會刪除任何嘢，所有狀態都以 JSON 檔案存在 git 入面，commit 歷史本身就係審計記錄。佢哋用 dry-run 模式同大量事前溝通確保第一次自動執行完全冇事故。

From 2,500 requests to 24: Rebuilding star history without the stargazers

https://github.blog/engineering/architecture-optimization/from-2500-requests-to-24-rebuilding-star-history-without-the-stargazers/📅 2026-10-15 03:11 UTC+8

GitHub has built a new API endpoint that returns up to 30 weeks of daily star counts in a single request, replacing the old method that forced tools to scrape thousands of individual stargazer records to reconstruct a project's growth curve. The change came after GitHub restricted stargazer list access to prevent spam and phishing, which broke tools like Star History and prompted a collaboration between GitHub's engineering team and the community to design a purpose-built solution. The new endpoint filters stars by date range before counting, cutting the requests needed for a repository like react/react from roughly 2,511 down to about 24 while eliminating the privacy risks of exposing user identities.

GitHub 而家起咗一個新嘅 API endpoint，一次過可以攞到最多 30 週嘅每日 star 數，唔使再好似以前咁逐頁 scrape 幾千條 stargazer 記錄先至畫到條增長曲線。之前 GitHub 為咗防止有人用 stargazer 名單做 spam 同 phishing，限制咗嗰個 endpoint 嘅存取權，搞到 Star History 呢啲工具用唔到，後來 GitHub 同社群合作設計咗呢個新方案。新 endpoint 嘅關鍵係先按日期範圍 filter 先至 count，所以好似 react/react 咁多 star 嘅 repo，以前要大約 2,511 個 request，而家只需要大約 24 個搞掂，同時都唔會再暴露用戶身份。

Building Git infrastructure for agent-scale development

https://github.blog/engineering/architecture-optimization/building-git-infrastructure-for-agent-scale-development/📅 2026-10-07 04:57 UTC+8

GitHub is rebuilding its Git infrastructure to handle the explosive growth in agentic software development, where total Git activity has more than doubled to 473 billion events per month and commits have grown over five times year on year. The current architecture, which ties durability and read scale to the same set of replicas, creates a ceiling at the highest activity levels because adding read capacity slows writes and losing quorum stops them entirely. The new design separates durability from scale so that thousands of agents pushing concurrently to a single repository no longer bottleneck each other, while preserving the reliability, controls, and workflows teams already depend on.

GitHub 而家要重新打造佢嘅 Git 基礎設施，因為 AI agent 開發令 Git 活動量一年內翻倍去到每月 4,733 億次事件，commit 數量更加升咗超過五倍。現有架構將持久化同讀取擴展綁喺同一組副本上面，一旦寫入量去到極高水準，加副本會拖慢寫入、少副本又會減讀取能力，形成一個天花板。新設計會將持久化同擴展分開處理，令幾千個 agent 同時 push 同一個 repo 唔會再互相拖慢，同時保留團隊已經依賴嘅可靠性同工作流程。

https://github.blog/engineering/architecture-optimization/improving-site-performance-by-shipping-more-css/📅 2026-09-25 23:00 UTC+8

GitHub's Primer Design System team migrated from CSS-in-JS to CSS Modules to address growing performance issues as component counts exploded across pages. The migration delivered significant gains — 55% faster server-side rendering and 25% faster component initialization — and required years of incremental work including feature flags, wrapper components, codemods, and eventually AI coding agents to migrate thousands of sx props across the codebase. The effort, spanning from 2023 to 2026, demonstrates how large-scale design system changes can be executed safely at scale.

GitHub 嘅 Primer 設計系統團隊為咗解決 CSS-in-JS 越嚟越慢嘅問題，花咗幾年時間逐步遷移去 CSS Modules，最終令伺服器端渲染快咗 55%、組件初始化快咗 25%。成個過程用咗 feature flag、wrapper 組件、codemod 仲有 Copilot coding agent 嚺處理幾千個 sx prop，由 2023 年一直做到 2026 年先搞掂。呢個案例話畀我哋知，大型設計系統嘅遷移雖然艱鉅，但用對策略就可以安全咁一步步完成。

Rendering huge pull requests in the GitHub Copilot app

https://github.blog/engineering/user-experience/rendering-huge-pull-requests-in-the-github-copilot-app/📅 2026-09-24 02:29 UTC+8

GitHub engineers rebuilt the pull request view in the Copilot app to stay fast even on extreme diffs, testing against an open-source pull request with 2,200 files, over a million changed lines, and more than 400 inline review comments. The key insight was splitting the layout into two independent geometry systems: a deterministic one for code rows whose heights are known upfront, and a lazily-measured one for review comments whose heights can only be determined at render time. This approach prevents the scroll jumps and jank that would otherwise make large pull requests unusable in a review workflow.

GitHub 嘅工程師重新設計咗 Copilot app 入面嘅 pull request 檢視介面，用一個有 2,200 個檔案、超過一百萬行改動同 400 多條 inline 評論嘅超大 pull request 做壓力測試。佢哋最核心嘅做法係將版面幾何分成兩套獨立系統：程式碼行用一套可以預先算好高度嘅確定性系統，而評論因為高度要 render 先知道，所以用另一套惰性量度嘅系統。呢個方法解決咗大 pull request 一滾就跳、一滾就窒嘅問題，令開發者可以順暢咗審閱巨型改動。

Don’t stop early: Case-folding source code at memory speed

https://github.blog/engineering/architecture-optimization/dont-stop-early-case-folding-source-code-at-memory-speed/📅 2026-08-01 00:00 UTC+8

GitHub's Blackbird code search engine, which indexes over 180 million repositories and 480TB of source code, needed case-folding to run at memory bandwidth speed, and the biggest performance win came from a counterintuitive move: removing the early-exit branch that bails out at the first non-ASCII byte. By eliminating all data-dependent branches from the hot loop, the compiler could vectorize the entire sweep, pushing throughput from roughly 3 GiB/s to over 45 GiB/s on an Apple M4. The result, open-sourced as a Rust crate called casefold, demonstrates that in tight loops the branch itself is the enemy, and a single branch-free pass beats even a cleverly fused two-pass approach.

GitHub 嘅 Blackbird 代碼搜尋引擎要處理超過 1.8 億個 repo、480TB 嘅原始碼，case-folding 呢個基本操作都必須跑到記憶體頻寬嘅速度，而最大嘅提速竟然係來自一個反直覺嘅決定：將遇到非 ASCII 字節就提前退出嘅 branch 完全刪走。刪走所有數據相關嘅分支之後，編譯器先可以將成個迴圈向量化，喺 Apple M4 上面由大約 3 GiB/s 直接衝到超過 45 GiB/s。佢哋將成果開源做咗一個叫 casefold 嘅 Rust crate，核心教訓就係喺熱迴圈入面 branch 先係真正嘅敵人，一條無分支嘅單次掃描比任何花巧嘅兩段式融合方案都要快。

Multi-region disaster recovery for Amazon API Gateway WebSocket APIs

https://aws.amazon.com/blogs/architecture/multi-region-disaster-recovery-for-amazon-api-gateway-websocket-apis/📅 2026-10-14 22:31 UTC+8

This article presents a design pattern for multi-region disaster recovery of Amazon API Gateway WebSocket APIs, combining Route 53 failover routing, DynamoDB Global Tables for cross-region state replication, and a canary health probe to detect regional failures automatically. When the primary region goes down, clients reconnect to a secondary region through the same domain and restore their session state without any configuration changes. The pattern is critical for real-time applications such as live auctions, collaborative editors, and IoT dashboards that rely on persistent bidirectional connections and shared mutable state.

呢篇文章介紹咗一個用 Amazon API Gateway WebSocket API 做跨區域災難恢復嘅設計模式，核心做法係用 Route 53 做 DNS 故障切換、用 DynamoDB Global Tables 將應用狀態即時複製到第二個區域，再配合一個 canary 健康探測器去自動偵測主區域有冇出事。一旦主區域掛咗，客戶端會自動重連到備用區域，用返同一個域名就搞掂，唔使改任何設定，session 狀態都唔會丟失。呢個方案對實時競投、協作編輯器、IoT 儀表板呢類需要持續雙向連接同共享狀態嘅應用嚟講特別重要。

https://magazine.sebastianraschka.com/p/llm-research-papers-2026-part1📅 2026-06-06 19:16 UTC+8

A researcher has published a curated list of notable LLM papers from January to May 2026, organized into categories spanning architecture, training efficiency, inference, reasoning, reinforcement learning, agent systems, and diffusion language models. The standout trend is the rise of hybrid architectures that alternate between standard attention and state-space layers, exemplified by Nvidia's Nemotron 3 series and Qwen3.6, driven largely by the need for efficient long-context handling in agentic applications. The list also reflects a broader shift in the field toward practical serving infrastructure, tool use, and coding agents rather than simply scaling model size.

一位研究者整理咗2026年一月到五月嘅LLM論文清單，涵蓋架構設計、訓練效率、推理、推理模型、強化學習、代理系統同擴散語言模型等範疇。今年最突出嘅趨勢係混合架構，就係將標準注意力層同狀態空間層交替排列，例如Nvidia嘅Nemotron 3同Qwen3.6，主要係因為代理應用需要處理越嚟越長嘅上下文。呢份清單亦反映咗整個領域嘅方向已經由單純堆大模型轉向實用嘅服務基礎設施、工具使用同編程代理。

Recent Developments in LLM Architectures: KV Sharing, mHC, and Compressed Attention

https://magazine.sebastianraschka.com/p/recent-developments-in-llm-architectures📅 2026-05-16 19:33 UTC+8

Recent open-weight LLM releases from Google, DeepSeek, and others are introducing architectural innovations aimed at reducing KV cache size and attention costs, which have become the primary bottlenecks as reasoning models and agent workflows demand longer contexts. Key techniques include cross-layer KV sharing in Gemma 4, per-layer embeddings for parameter efficiency, compressed convolutional attention in ZAYA1, and mHC with compressed attention in DeepSeek V4. These changes represent a shift toward treating memory and compute efficiency as first-class design priorities rather than afterthoughts.

最近Google、DeepSeek同其他公司推出嘅開源大模型，都喺架構上做咗好多新嘢，主要係想減少KV cache嘅大小同attention嘅運算成本，因為而家reasoning model同agent workflow都需要處理好長嘅context。比較突出嘅技術包括Gemma 4嘅跨層KV sharing、per-layer embeddings、ZAYA1嘅compressed convolutional attention，同埋DeepSeek V4嘅mHC加compressed attention。呢啲改動反映咗一個趨勢，就係記憶體同運算效率已經唔再係事後補救，而係由頭開始就係設計嘅核心考量。

https://magazine.sebastianraschka.com/p/beyond-standard-llms📅 2025-11-04 21:06 UTC+8

It highlights a key tension in the field: while linear attention promises significant efficiency gains for long sequences, MiniMax's decision to revert to standard attention in their M2 model due to poor reasoning and multi-turn performance suggests the technology is not yet production-ready. The piece serves as an introductory overview of these emerging approaches, following up on the author's talk at PyTorch Conference 2025.

呢篇文章介紹咗而家喺標準 transformer LLM 以外嘅一啲替代方案，特別係最近重新興起嘅 linear attention 機制，例如 MiniMax-M1、Qwen3-Next、DeepSeek V3.2 同 Kimi Linear 呢啲模型。值得留意嘅係 MiniMax 喺佢哋嘅 M2 模型入面反而轉返用回傳統 attention，因為佢哋發現 linear attention 喺推理同多輪對話任務上面表現唔好，即係話呢個技術仲未完全成熟。整體嚟講，呢篇文係一個入門性質嘅綜述，幫讀者了解呢啲新方向嘅發展同取捨。

From GPT-2 to gpt-oss: Analyzing the Architectural Advances

https://magazine.sebastianraschka.com/p/from-gpt-2-to-gpt-oss-analyzing-the📅 2025-08-09 19:23 UTC+8

OpenAI has released gpt-oss-120b and gpt-oss-20b, their first open-weight large language models since GPT-2 in 2019, marking a significant return to open research after years of closed development. The models build on the transformer architecture with refinements such as RoPE positional encoding, Swish activation functions, and MXFP4 quantization that allows the 20B model to run on a consumer GPU with 16 GB of RAM. The release matters because it offers researchers and developers a rare look inside a frontier-class model's design choices, while also demonstrating that OpenAI still sees value in contributing to the open ecosystem.

OpenAI 終於推出咗 gpt-oss-120b 同 gpt-oss-20b，呢兩個係佢哋由 2019 年 GPT-2 之後首次公開權重嘅大型語言模型，代表佢哋重新投入開放研究嘅重要一步。呢啲模型雖然仲係用 transformer 架構，但加入咗 RoPE 位置編碼、Swish 激活函數同 MXFP4 量化技術，令 20B 模型可以喺 16GB 顯卡上面跑。呢次發布之所以重要，係因為佢俾研究者同開發者一個難得嘅機會去睇清楚前沿模型嘅設計思路，同時都話畀業界知 OpenAI 仲係願意為開放生態做出貢獻。
